- **global-options**: Every command takes `-q`, `-v`, `--json`, `--progress`, `--offline`, `--yes`, `--color`, `-C &lt;dir&gt;`, `--mirror &lt;url&gt;` and `--no-config`. `add`, `get`, `remove`, `exclude`, `sync`, `lock`, `push` and `cache gc` also take `-n`, `--dry-run`. `add` and `get` also take `--select &lt;glob&gt;`, `--exclude &lt;glob&gt;`, `--no-extract` and `-i &lt;file&gt;`, which reads links from a list. `add` takes `--as &lt;name&gt;` to name ([nikhil-yn-aix/fetchloom](https://github.com/nikhil-yn-aix/fetchloom/blob/HEAD/docs/user/commands/global-options.md))
